## 12:41 CDT — Project Shiesty pre-fire correction
- **UPDATE replacing any implication that an earlier Shiesty wrapper was still running/armed:** `pgrep` found no active `claim-with-tinfoil` / `fast-mint` process. The timed-out ~11:05 worker did not leave a persistent fire process; no Shiesty tx or claim has been verified yet.
- Two manual `fast-mint-fork.mjs` attempts with `--mint-url https://projectshiesty.xyz`, `--route-site`, and `--route-x` exited before pre-encode/sign/broadcast because the nested Tinfoil route gate could not find route evidence. Root cause: `route-gate.mjs` searches the pipeline's `projects` array, while the verified Shiesty route was only in the top-level `projectshiesty` entry. The matching `projects[51]` entry was updated with contract, route, `route_verified:true`, and armed status.
- After that pipeline repair, the next manual attempt passed the route gate but Tinfoil returned **SUSPICIOUS** on the site bundle (`eip7702` and `personal_sign` patterns), so it correctly refused the claim; no wallet action occurred.
- A later direct `scan-url.mjs` check returned **CLEAN / 0**, but fetched **0 of 1 script bodies**, so that result alone was incomplete. Separately, the 12:35 foilbox handoff reported a CLEAN Tinfoil scan at 17:32:57Z and the 12:40 handoff kept Shiesty armed for the ~17:50Z pre-encode run using direct `fast-mint-fork.mjs` without `--mint-url` (the already-completed preflight basis).
